T_{\delta}\coloneqq T_{\epsilon}+T_{1}+\frac{\ln(1/b_{\delta})}{2\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}+\frac{\ln({1}/{\widetilde{\delta}})}{2\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}
. Next, since
\|\bm{\psi}\left(T_{\delta},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)\|_{2}\leq B+\epsilon/2\leq 2B
, using
eq.

61
and
eq.

62
, we get

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla\mathcal{L}\left(\bm{\psi}\left(T_{\delta},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\nabla\mathcal{L}({\mathbf{p}}(T_{\epsilon}))\right\|_{2}+\left\|\nabla\mathcal{L}\left(\bm{\psi}\left(T_{\delta},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)\right)-\nabla\mathcal{L}({\mathbf{p}}(T_{\epsilon}))\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq{\epsilon}/{2}+\widetilde{A}\widetilde{C}\delta^{\frac{\Delta}{\Delta+8\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}}\leq\epsilon,

where the final inequality is true for all sufficiently small
\delta>0
, thus completing the proof.
∎

Proof.

of Corollary
6
:
Since
\lim_{t\to\infty}{\mathbf{p}}(t)/\|{\mathbf{p}}(t)\|_{2}={\mathbf{p}}^{*}
,
\lim_{t\to\infty}\|{\mathbf{p}}(t)\|_{2}=\infty
, and
\lim_{t\to\infty}\nabla\mathcal{L}({\mathbf{p}}(t))=\mathbf{0}
, therefore, for any
\epsilon\in(0,1)
, we can choose a
T_{\epsilon}
such that

{\mathbf{p}}(T_{\epsilon})^{\top}{\mathbf{p}}^{*}/\|{\mathbf{p}}(T_{\epsilon})\|_{2}\geq 1-\epsilon/2,\|{\mathbf{p}}(T_{\epsilon})\|_{2}\geq 1/\epsilon,\text{ and }\|\nabla\mathcal{L}({\mathbf{p}}(T_{\epsilon}))\|_{2}\leq\epsilon/2.

(63)

Let
B_{\epsilon}\coloneqq\max_{t\in[0,T_{\epsilon}]}\|{\mathbf{p}}(t)\|_{2}
. Then, since
\mathcal{L}(\cdot)
has locally Lipschitz gradient, there exists a constant
\widetilde{A}_{\epsilon}>0
such that, if
\|{\mathbf{w}}_{1}\|_{2},\|{\mathbf{w}}_{2}\|_{2}\leq B_{\epsilon}+\epsilon
, it follows that

\|\nabla\mathcal{L}({\mathbf{w}}_{1})-\nabla\mathcal{L}({\mathbf{w}}_{2})\|_{2}\leq\widetilde{A}_{\epsilon}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{2}\|_{2},

(64)

where note that
\widetilde{A}_{\epsilon}
depends on
\epsilon
. Since
T_{\epsilon}
does not depend on
\delta
, from Theorem
4
, for all sufficiently small
\delta>0
and for all
t\in[-T_{\epsilon},T_{\epsilon}]
,

\left\|\bm{\psi}\left(t+T_{1}+\frac{\ln(1/b_{\delta})}{2\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}+\frac{\ln({1}/{\widetilde{\delta}})}{2\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)-{\mathbf{p}}(t)\right\|_{2}\leq\widetilde{C}\delta^{\frac{\Delta}{\Delta+8\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}}\leq\epsilon/2.

(65)

Putting
t=T_{\epsilon}
in the above equation, and using
eq.

63
, we get, for all sufficiently small
\delta
,

\left\|\bm{\psi}\left(T_{\delta},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)\right\|_{2}\geq\|{\mathbf{p}}(T_{\epsilon})\|_{2}-\widetilde{C}\delta^{\frac{\Delta}{\Delta+8\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}}\geq{1}/{\epsilon}-\widetilde{C}\delta^{\frac{\Delta}{\Delta+8\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}}\geq{1}/{(2\epsilon)},

where
T_{\delta}\coloneqq T_{\epsilon}+T_{1}+\frac{\ln(1/b_{\delta})}{2\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}+\frac{\ln({1}/{\widetilde{\delta}})}{2\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}
. We also have

\frac{\bm{\psi}\left(T_{\delta},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)^{\top}{\mathbf{p}}^{*}}{\left\|\bm{\psi}\left(T_{\delta},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)\right\|_{2}}\geq\frac{{\mathbf{p}}(T_{\epsilon})^{\top}{\mathbf{p}}_{*}-\widetilde{C}\delta^{\frac{\Delta}{\Delta+8\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}}}{\left\|\bm{\psi}\left(T_{\delta},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)\right\|_{2}}\geq\frac{(1-\epsilon/2)\|{\mathbf{p}}(T_{\epsilon})\|_{2}-\widetilde{C}\delta^{\frac{\Delta}{\Delta+8\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{p}}(T_{\epsilon})\right\|_{2}+\widetilde{C}\delta^{\frac{\Delta}{\Delta+8\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}}}\geq 1-\epsilon,

where the first inequality uses
eq.

65
. The second inequality uses
eq.

65
and
eq.

63
. The final inequality is true for all sufficiently small
\delta>0
. Next, since
\|\bm{\psi}\left(T_{\delta},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)\|_{2}\leq B_{\epsilon}+\epsilon/2
, using
eq.

64
and
eq.

63
, we get

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla\mathcal{L}\left(\bm{\psi}\left(T_{\delta},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\nabla\mathcal{L}({\mathbf{p}}(T_{\epsilon}))\right\|_{2}+\left\|\nabla\mathcal{L}\left(\bm{\psi}\left(T_{\delta},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)\right)-\nabla\mathcal{L}({\mathbf{p}}(T_{\epsilon}))\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq{\epsilon}/{2}+\widetilde{A}_{\epsilon}\widetilde{C}\delta^{\frac{\Delta}{\Delta+8\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}}\leq\epsilon,

where the final inequality is true for all sufficiently small
\delta>0
, thus completing the proof.
∎

Appendix C
Proof Omitted from
Section

3.2

We first prove three important lemmata, which are then used to prove
Theorem

11
.

Lemma 31
.

Consider the setting in
Theorem

11
. Suppose there exists a constant
C_{1}>0
such that for every
\delta>0
,
{{\mathbf{a}}}_{\delta}
is a vector that satisfies
\|{{\mathbf{a}}}_{\delta}-\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\|_{2}\leq C_{1}\delta^{L+1}
. Then, for any fixed
\widetilde{T}\in(-\infty,\infty)
, there exists a constant
C>0
such that for all sufficiently small
\delta>0
,

\left\|\bm{\psi}\left(t+\frac{1/\delta^{L-2}}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},{{\mathbf{a}}}_{\delta}\right)-\bm{\psi}\left(t+\frac{1/\delta^{L-2}}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},\delta{{\mathbf{w}}}_{*}\right)\right\|_{2}\leq C\delta,\text{ for all }t\in[-\widetilde{T},\widetilde{T}].

Proof.

We choose
\gamma>0
sufficiently small such that for all unit-norm vector
{\mathbf{w}}
satisfying
{\mathbf{w}}^{\top}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\geq 1-\gamma
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}})\leq\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*}),\|\nabla^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}})\|_{2}\leq L(L-1/2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*}),\text{ and }

(66)

\displaystyle(\nabla\mathcal{N}(t_{1}{\mathbf{w}})-\nabla\mathcal{N}(t_{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}))^{\top}\left(t_{1}{\mathbf{w}}-t_{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right)\leq L(L-1)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})t_{2}^{L-2}\|t_{1}{\mathbf{w}}-t_{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\|_{2}^{2},\forall t_{2}\geq t_{1}\geq 0.

(67)

The first inequality follows from Lemma
24
. The second inequality holds since, from Lemma
21
,
\|\nabla^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})\|_{2}=L(L-1)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})
, and
\nabla^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}})
is continuous in the neighborhood of
{\mathbf{w}}_{*}
. The third inequality follows from Lemma
23
.

We further define
f({\mathbf{w}})=\mathcal{J}({\mathbf{X}};{\mathbf{w}})^{\top}(\ell^{\prime}(\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{X}};{\mathbf{w}}),{\mathbf{y}})-\ell^{\prime}(\mathbf{0},{\mathbf{y}}))
. Then, note that

\|f({\mathbf{w}})\|_{2}\leq\|\mathcal{J}({\mathbf{X}};{\mathbf{w}})\|_{2}\|(\ell^{\prime}(\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{X}};{\mathbf{w}}),{\mathbf{y}})-\ell^{\prime}(\mathbf{0},{\mathbf{y}})\|_{2}\leq Kn\|\mathcal{J}({\mathbf{X}};{\mathbf{w}})\|_{2}\|\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{X}};{\mathbf{w}}),{\mathbf{y}})\|_{2},

where the first inequality follows from the Cauchy-Schwartz inequality, and the second follows from the smoothness of the loss function (see
Assumption

2
). Note that the RHS in the above inequality is
(2L-1)-
homogeneous in
{\mathbf{w}}
. Thus, there exists a
\beta>0
such that

\|f({\mathbf{w}})\|_{2}\leq\beta\|{\mathbf{w}}\|_{2}^{2L-1},\text{ for all }{\mathbf{w}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{k}.

(68)

Also,
f({\mathbf{w}})
is continuously differentiable in the neighborhood of
{\mathbf{w}}_{*}
. Thus, in a similar way as in the proof of Lemma
10
, we may assume that for all vectors
{\mathbf{w}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{k}
that satisfy
{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{w}}/\|{\mathbf{w}}\|_{2}\geq 1-\gamma,
we have

\|\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})\|_{2}\leq K\sum_{i=1}^{n}\|\nabla^{2}\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{x}}_{i};{\mathbf{w}})\|_{2}|\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{x}}_{i};{\mathbf{w}})|+\|\nabla\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{x}}_{i};{\mathbf{w}})\nabla\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{x}}_{i};{\mathbf{w}})^{\top}\|_{2}.

Note that the final upper bound in the above equation is
(2L-2)
-homogeneous. Thus, there exists a
\zeta>0
such that for all vector
{\mathbf{w}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{k}
satisfying
{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{w}}/\|{\mathbf{w}}\|_{2}\geq 1-\gamma,
we have

\|\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})\|_{2}\leq\zeta\|{\mathbf{w}}\|_{2}^{2L-2}.

Furthermore, from the mean value theorem, we have

\displaystyle\|f({\mathbf{w}}_{1})-f({\mathbf{w}}_{2})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla f(\tilde{{\mathbf{w}}})\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{2}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\zeta\max(\|{\mathbf{w}}_{2}\|_{2}^{2L-2},\|{\mathbf{w}}_{1}\|_{2}^{2L-2})\|{\mathbf{w}}_{1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{2}\|_{2},

(69)

where
{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{w}}_{1}/\|{\mathbf{w}}_{1}\|_{2},{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{w}}_{2}/\|{\mathbf{w}}_{2}\|_{2}\geq 1-\gamma.

Now, let
{\mathbf{q}}(t)=\bm{\psi}(t,{\mathbf{a}}_{\delta})
and
{\mathbf{u}}(t)=\bm{\psi}(t,\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{*})
. Then
\|{\mathbf{q}}(0)-{\mathbf{u}}(0)\|_{2}\leq C_{1}\delta^{L+1},
which implies
\|{\mathbf{q}}(0)\|_{2}\leq\delta+C_{1}\delta^{L+1}
. Therefore, for all sufficiently small
\delta>0
, we have

\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{q}}(0)}{\|{\mathbf{q}}(0)\|_{2}}=\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}(0)+{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}({\mathbf{q}}(0)-{\mathbf{u}}(0))}{\|{\mathbf{q}}(0)\|_{2}}\geq\frac{\delta-C_{1}\delta^{L+1}}{\|{\mathbf{q}}(0)\|_{2}}\geq\frac{\delta-C_{1}\delta^{L+1}}{\delta+C_{1}\delta^{L+1}}>1-\gamma.

Define

\overline{T}_{1}=\min_{t\geq 0}\left\{t:\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{q}}(t)}{\|{\mathbf{q}}(t)\|_{2}}=1-\gamma\right\}.

Recall that
{\mathbf{q}}(t)
satisfies

\dot{{\mathbf{q}}}=-\mathcal{J}({\mathbf{X}};{\mathbf{q}})^{\top}\ell^{\prime}(\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{X}};{\mathbf{q}}),{\mathbf{y}})=\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{q}})-\mathcal{J}({\mathbf{X}};{\mathbf{q}})^{\top}(\ell^{\prime}(\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{X}};{\mathbf{q}}),{\mathbf{y}})-\ell^{\prime}(\mathbf{0},{\mathbf{y}})).

Multiplying the above equation by
{\mathbf{q}}^{\top}
from the left we get

\frac{1}{2}\frac{d\|{\mathbf{q}}\|_{2}^{2}}{dt}=L\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{q}})-L\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{X}};{\mathbf{q}})^{\top}(\ell^{\prime}(\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{X}};{\mathbf{q}}),{\mathbf{y}})-\ell^{\prime}(\mathbf{0},{\mathbf{y}}))\leq L\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{q}})=L\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{q}}/\|{\mathbf{q}}\|_{2})\|{\mathbf{q}}\|_{2}^{L},

where the first equality follows from Lemma
20
, the first inequality is due to convexity of the loss function, and the second equality holds since
\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{q}})
is
L
-homogeneous.

Now, since
{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{q}}(t)/\|{\mathbf{q}}(t)\|_{2}\geq 1-\gamma,
for all
t\in[0,\overline{T}_{1}]
, from
eq.

66
, we get

\frac{d\|{\mathbf{q}}\|_{2}^{2}}{dt}\leq L\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})\|{\mathbf{q}}\|_{2}^{L},

which implies

\|{\mathbf{q}}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}\leq\frac{\|{\mathbf{q}}(0)\|_{2}^{L-2}}{1-t\|{\mathbf{q}}(0)\|_{2}^{L-2}L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},\text{ for all }t\in[0,\overline{T}_{1}].

Define
{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)=\frac{1}{\delta}{\mathbf{q}}\left(\frac{t}{\delta^{L-2}}\right)
and
\eta=(1+C_{1}\delta^{L})^{L-2}
, then, for all
t\in[0,\overline{T}_{1}]
,

\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}\leq\frac{(1+C_{1}\delta^{L})^{L-2}}{1-t(1+C_{1}\delta^{L})^{L-2}L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}=\frac{\eta}{1-t\eta L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},

(70)

where we used
\|{\mathbf{q}}(0)\|_{2}\leq\delta(1+C_{1}\delta^{L})
. Also, since

\displaystyle\frac{d{\mathbf{s}}_{q}}{dt}=\frac{1}{\delta^{L-1}}\dot{{\mathbf{q}}}\left(\frac{t}{\delta^{L-2}}\right)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\delta^{L-1}}\mathcal{J}\left({\mathbf{X}};{\mathbf{q}}\left(\frac{t}{\delta^{L-2}}\right)\right)^{\top}\ell^{\prime}\left(\mathcal{H}\left({\mathbf{X}};{\mathbf{q}}\left(\frac{t}{\delta^{L-2}}\right)\right),{\mathbf{y}}\right)

\displaystyle=-\mathcal{J}\left({\mathbf{X}};\frac{1}{\delta}{\mathbf{q}}\left(\frac{t}{\delta^{L-2}}\right)\right)^{\top}\ell^{\prime}\left(\mathcal{H}\left({\mathbf{X}};{\mathbf{q}}\left(\frac{t}{\delta^{L-2}}\right)\right),{\mathbf{y}}\right)

\displaystyle=-\mathcal{J}\left({\mathbf{X}};{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)\right)^{\top}\ell^{\prime}\left(\mathcal{H}\left({\mathbf{X}};\delta{\mathbf{s}}_{q}\left(t\right)\right),{\mathbf{y}}\right),

where the second equality follows from
(L-1)
-homogeneity of
\mathcal{J}\left({\mathbf{X}};{\mathbf{w}}\right)
, we have that
{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)
is the solution of

\dot{{\mathbf{s}}}=\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{s}})-\mathcal{J}({\mathbf{X}};{\mathbf{s}})^{\top}(\ell^{\prime}(\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{X}};\delta{\mathbf{s}}),{\mathbf{y}})-\ell^{\prime}(\mathbf{0},{\mathbf{y}}))=\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{s}})-f(\delta{\mathbf{s}})/\delta^{L-1},{\mathbf{s}}(0)={\mathbf{q}}(0)/\delta.

Let
{\mathbf{z}}(t)=\frac{\eta^{\frac{1}{L-2}}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}}{(1-t\eta L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*}))^{1/(L-2)}}
, which is the solution of

\dot{{\mathbf{z}}}=\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{z}}),{\mathbf{z}}(0)=\eta^{\frac{1}{L-2}}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}.

Note that, for
t\in[0,\overline{T}_{1}]
,

\displaystyle\left(\nabla\mathcal{N}\left({{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)}\right)-\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{z}}(t))\right)^{\top}\left({{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)}-{\mathbf{z}}(t)\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\nabla\mathcal{N}\left({\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)\|_{2}}\frac{{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)\|_{2}}\right)-\nabla\mathcal{N}\left({{\mathbf{z}}(t)}\right)\right)^{\top}\left({\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)\|_{2}}\frac{{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)\|_{2}}-{{\mathbf{z}}(t)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq L(L-1)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})\|{\mathbf{z}}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}\left\|{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)\|_{2}}\frac{{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)\|_{2}}-\|{\mathbf{z}}(t)\|_{2}\frac{{\mathbf{z}}(t)}{\|{\mathbf{z}}(t)\|_{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=L(L-1)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})\|{\mathbf{z}}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}\left\|{{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)}-{{\mathbf{z}}(t)}\right\|^{2},

(71)

where the inequality follows since, from
eq.

70
,
{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)\|_{2}}\leq\|{\mathbf{z}}(t)\|_{2}
,
{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{q}}(t)/\|{\mathbf{q}}(t)\|_{2}\geq 1-\gamma
for all
t\in[0,\overline{T}_{1}]
, and from
eq.

41
. Hence, for
t\in[0,\overline{T}_{1}]
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)-{\mathbf{z}}(t)\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left({\mathbf{s}}_{q}-{\mathbf{z}}\right)^{\top}\left(\dot{{\mathbf{s}}}_{q}-\dot{{\mathbf{z}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\left({\mathbf{s}}_{q}-{\mathbf{z}}\right)^{\top}\left(\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{s}}_{q})-\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{z}})\right)-\left({\mathbf{s}}_{q}-{\mathbf{z}}\right)^{\top}f(\delta{\mathbf{s}}_{q})/\delta^{L-1}

\displaystyle\leq L(L-1)

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})\|{\mathbf{z}}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)-{\mathbf{z}}(t)\right\|_{2}^{2}+\beta\delta^{L}\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)-{\mathbf{z}}(t)\right\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)\|_{2}^{2L-1}.

Next, since for all
t\in[0,\overline{T}_{1}]
,

\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}\leq\|{\mathbf{z}}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}=\frac{\eta}{1-t\eta L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},

(72)

we have

\frac{d}{dt}\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)-{\mathbf{z}}(t)\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{\eta L(L-1)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}{(1-t\eta L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*}))}\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)-{\mathbf{z}}(t)\right\|_{2}+\beta\delta^{L}\|{\mathbf{z}}(t)\|_{2}^{2L-1}.

Using Lemma
30
, we get

\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)-{\mathbf{z}}(t)\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{1}{P(t)}\left(\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(0)-{\mathbf{z}}(0)\right\|_{2}+\beta\delta^{L}\int_{0}^{t}P(s)\|{\mathbf{z}}(t)\|_{2}^{2L-1}ds\right),

where
P(t)=e^{-\int_{0}^{t}b(s)ds}
and
b(t)=\frac{\eta L(L-1)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}{(1-t\eta L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*}))}
. Using Lemma
29
, we have

P(t)=\left(1-t\eta L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})\right)^{(L-1)/(L-2)},

which implies

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}P(s)\|{\mathbf{z}}(s)\|_{2}^{2L-1}ds

\displaystyle\leq\eta^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}\int_{0}^{t}\left(1-s\eta L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})\right)^{\frac{L-1}{L-2}-\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}ds

\displaystyle=\eta^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}\int_{0}^{t}1/\left(1-s\eta L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})\right)^{\frac{L}{L-2}}ds

\displaystyle=\eta^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}\frac{L-2}{2\eta L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}\left(\frac{1}{\left(1-t\eta L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})\right)^{\frac{2}{L-2}}}-1\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta^{\frac{L+1}{L-2}}}{2L\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}\left(\frac{1}{\left(1-t\eta L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})\right)^{\frac{2}{L-2}}}\right).

Hence, for all
t\in[0,\overline{T}_{1}]
and all sufficiently small
\delta>0
,

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)-{\mathbf{z}}(t)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(0)-{\mathbf{z}}(0)\right\|_{2}}{\left(1-t\eta L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})\right)^{\frac{L-1}{L-2}}}+\frac{\beta\delta^{L}\eta^{\frac{L+1}{L-2}}}{2L\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}\left(\frac{1}{\left(1-t\eta L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})\right)^{\frac{L+1}{L-2}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{2}\delta_{2}^{L}}{\left(1-t\eta L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})\right)^{\frac{L+1}{L-2}}},

(73)

where
C_{2}
is a sufficiently large constant, and the last inequality follows since
\left(1-t\eta L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})\right)^{\frac{L+1}{L-2}}\leq\left(1-t\eta L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})\right)^{\frac{L-1}{L-2}}
and, for all sufficiently small
\delta>0
,

\displaystyle\left\|{{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(0)}-{\mathbf{z}}(0)\right\|_{2}=\left\|\frac{{\mathbf{a}}_{\delta}}{\delta}-(1+C_{1}\delta^{L}){\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|\frac{{\mathbf{a}}_{\delta}}{\delta}-{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right\|_{2}+\left\|C_{1}\delta^{L}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right\|_{2}=O(\delta^{L}).

Let
\tau_{q}(t)={\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)-{\mathbf{z}}(t)
, and define

h_{1}(t)=\frac{\eta^{1/(L-2)}}{\left(1-t\eta L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})\right)^{1/(L-2)}},h_{2}(t)=\frac{1}{\left(1-tL(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})\right)}.

By definition of
\overline{T}_{1}
,

\displaystyle 1-\gamma=\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(\overline{T}_{1})}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(\overline{T}_{1})\|_{2}}\geq\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(\overline{T}_{1})}{h_{1}(\overline{T}_{1})}=\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{z}}(\overline{T}_{1})+{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}\tau_{q}(\overline{T}_{1})}{h_{1}(\overline{T}_{1})}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{h_{1}(\overline{T}_{1})-\|\tau_{p}(\overline{T}_{1})\|_{2}}{h_{1}(\overline{T}_{1})}

\displaystyle\geq 1-

\displaystyle\frac{C_{2}\delta^{L}/\eta^{1/(L-2)}}{\left(1-\overline{T}_{1}\eta L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})\right)^{\frac{L}{L-2}}},

which implies, for some sufficiently large constant
\widetilde{C}_{2}
,

\displaystyle 1-\eta\overline{T}_{1}L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})\leq\frac{C_{2}^{\frac{L-2}{L}}\delta^{L-2}/\eta^{1/L}}{\gamma^{\frac{L-2}{L}}}\leq\widetilde{C}_{2}\delta^{L-2}.

Therefore, there exists a sufficiently large constant
{C}_{3}
such that

\displaystyle\overline{T}_{1}\geq\frac{1-\widetilde{C}_{2}\delta^{L-2}}{\eta L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}\geq\frac{1-{C}_{3}\delta^{L-2}}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},

where the second inequality follows since, for all sufficiently small
\delta>0
,

\displaystyle\frac{1-\widetilde{C}_{2}\delta^{L-2}}{(1+C_{1}\delta^{L})^{L-2}}\geq\frac{1-\widetilde{C}_{2}\delta^{L-2}}{(1+2C_{1}(L-2)\delta^{L})}\geq(1-\widetilde{C}_{2}\delta^{L-2})(1-2C_{1}(L-2)\delta^{L})\geq 1-C_{3}\delta^{L-2},

where
{C}_{3}
is a sufficiently large constant. Hence,

\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)\|_{2}}\geq 1-\gamma,\text{ for all }t\in\left[0,\frac{\left(1-\delta^{L-2}C_{3}\right)}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}\right].

(74)

Next, recall
{\mathbf{u}}(t)=\bm{\psi}(t,\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{*})
, and define
{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)=\frac{1}{\delta}{\mathbf{u}}\left(\frac{t}{\delta^{L-2}}\right)
. Note that

\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(0)}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(0)\|_{2}}=1>1-\gamma.

Define

\overline{T}_{3}\coloneqq\min_{t\geq 0}\left\{t:\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)\|_{2}}=1-\gamma\right\}.

Then, following in a similar way as in the proof of
{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)
above, we can show that there exists a sufficiently large constant
C_{4}
such that, for all sufficiently small
\delta>0
,

\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)\|_{2}}\geq 1-\gamma,\text{ for all }t\in\left[0,\frac{\left(1-\delta^{L-2}C_{4}\right)}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}\right],\text{ and }

\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}\leq\frac{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(0)\|_{2}^{L-2}}{1-t\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(0)\|_{2}^{L-2}L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}=\frac{1}{1-tL(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}.

Now, choose
C_{5}
large enough such that
C_{5}^{L/(L-2)}>\zeta 2^{(2L-2)/(L-2)}/(L\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*}))
and
C_{5}\geq\max(C_{3},C_{4})
, and define
\overline{T}_{4}\coloneqq\frac{\left(1-\delta^{L-2}C_{5}\right)}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}
. Then, for all
t\in\left[0,\overline{T}_{4}\right]
, we have

\displaystyle{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)}/{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)\|_{2}},{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)}/{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)\|_{2}}\geq 1-\gamma,

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}\leq\frac{1}{1-tL(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}\leq\frac{1}{C_{5}\delta^{L-2}},\text{ and }

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}\leq\frac{\eta}{1-t\eta L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}=\frac{1}{1/\eta-1+C_{5}\delta^{L-2}}\leq\frac{2}{C_{5}\delta^{L-2}},

where the last inequality follows since, for all sufficiently small
\delta>0
,

\frac{1}{\eta}=\frac{1}{(1+C_{1}\delta^{L})^{L-2}}\geq\frac{1}{1+2C_{1}(L-2)\delta^{L}}\geq 1-2C_{1}(L-2)\delta^{L}\geq 1-C_{5}\delta^{L-2}/2.

(75)

Therefore, for
t\in[0,\overline{T}_{4}]
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\left\|{{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)}-{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)\right\|_{2}^{2}=\left({{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)}-{\mathbf{s}}_{u}\right)^{\top}\left({\dot{{\mathbf{s}}}_{q}}-\dot{{\mathbf{s}}}_{u}\right)

\displaystyle=\left({{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)}-{\mathbf{s}}_{u}\right)^{\top}\left(\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{s}}_{q})-\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{s}}_{u})\right)-\left({{\mathbf{s}}_{q}}-{\mathbf{s}}_{u}\right)^{\top}\left(f(\delta{\mathbf{s}}_{q})-f(\delta{\mathbf{s}}_{u})\right)/\delta^{L-1}

\displaystyle\leq(L(L-1/2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*}))\left\|{{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)}-{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)\right\|_{2}^{2}\max(\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2},\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2})+

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \zeta\delta^{L}\left\|{{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)}-{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)\right\|_{2}^{2}\max(\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)\|_{2}^{2L-2},\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)\|_{2}^{2L-2})

\displaystyle\leq\left(L(L-1/2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\frac{\zeta 2^{L/(L-2)}}{C_{5}^{L/(L-2)}}\right)\left\|{{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)}-{\mathbf{s}}_{u}\right\|_{2}^{2}\max(\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2},\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2})

\displaystyle\leq L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})\left\|{{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)}-{\mathbf{s}}_{u}\right\|_{2}^{2}\max(\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2},\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}),

where the first inequality follows from
eq.

66
,
\nabla^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}})
being
(L-2)
-homogeneous and
eq.

69
. The second inequality uses
\delta^{L}\max(\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)\|_{2}^{L},\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)\|_{2}^{L})\leq 2^{L/(L-2)}/C_{5}^{L/(L-2)}
, for all
t\in[0,\overline{T}_{4}]
. The last inequality follows from our assumption on
C_{5}.
Now, let
\kappa=L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})
, then

\frac{d}{dt}\left\|{{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)}-{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{\kappa\eta\left\|{{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)}-{\mathbf{s}}_{u}\right\|_{2}}{1-t\eta L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}.

Using Lemma
30
, we get

\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(t)-{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{1}{P_{1}(t)}\left(\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(0)-{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(0)\right\|_{2}\right),

where
P_{1}(t)=e^{-\int_{0}^{t}b_{1}(s)ds}
and
b_{1}(t)=\frac{\eta\kappa}{(1-t\eta L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*}))}
. From Lemma
29
, we know

P_{1}(t)=\left(1-t\eta L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})\right)^{L/(L-2)}.

Now, using
eq.

75
, there exists a constant
C_{6}
such that

P_{1}(\overline{T}_{4})=\left(1-\eta(1-\delta^{L-2}C_{5})\right)^{L/(L-2)}\geq\left(\frac{\eta C_{5}\delta^{L-2}}{2}\right)^{L/(L-2)}\geq C_{6}\delta^{L}.

Hence,

\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{q}(\overline{T}_{4})-{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(\overline{T}_{4})\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{1}{C_{6}\delta^{L}}\left(\left\|{\mathbf{a}}_{\delta}/\delta-{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right\|_{2}\right)\leq C_{1}/C_{6},

which implies

\left\|\bm{\psi}\left(\frac{\left({1}/{\delta^{L-2}}-C_{5}\right)}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},{{\mathbf{a}}}_{\delta}\right)-\bm{\psi}\left(\frac{\left({1}/{\delta^{L-2}}-C_{5}\right)}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},\delta{{\mathbf{w}}}_{*}\right)\right\|_{2}\leq C_{1}\delta/C_{6}.

Since
\widetilde{T}
is fixed, from Lemma
27
and the above inequality, there exists a
C>0
such that for all sufficiently small
\delta
, we have

\left\|\bm{\psi}\left(t+\frac{1}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{L-2}}\right),{{\mathbf{a}}}_{\delta}\right)-\bm{\psi}\left(t+\frac{1}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{L-2}}\right),\delta{{\mathbf{w}}}_{*}\right)\right\|_{2}\leq C\delta,

for all
t\in[-\widetilde{T},\widetilde{T}]
, which completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 32
.

Consider the setting of
Theorem

4
. For any fixed
t\in(-\infty,\infty)
and all sufficiently small
\delta_{2}\geq\delta_{1}>0
, there exists a
C>0
such that

\left\|\bm{\psi}\left(t+\frac{{1}/{\delta_{1}^{L-2}}}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},\delta_{1}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right)-\bm{\psi}\left(t+\frac{{1}/{\delta_{2}^{L-2}}}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},\delta_{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right)\right\|_{2}\leq C\delta_{2},

which implies
{\mathbf{p}}(t)
exists for all
t\in(-\infty,\infty)
. Furthermore, let
\delta_{1}\rightarrow 0
and
\delta_{2}=\delta
, then

\left\|{\mathbf{p}}(t)-\bm{\psi}\left(t+\frac{{1}/{\delta^{L-2}}}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right)\right\|_{2}\leq C\delta.

Also,
\mathcal{L}({\mathbf{p}}(0))\leq\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{0})-\eta
, for some
\eta>0
.

Proof.

We choose
\gamma>0
sufficiently small such that for all unit-norm vectors
{\mathbf{w}}
satisfying
{\mathbf{w}}^{\top}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\geq 1-\gamma
, we have
\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}})\leq\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})
, and for all
t_{2}\geq t_{1}\geq 0,

\left(\nabla\mathcal{N}(t_{1}{\mathbf{w}})-\nabla\mathcal{N}(t_{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{*})\right)^{\top}\left(t_{1}{\mathbf{w}}-t_{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right)\leq L(L-1)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})t_{2}^{L-2}\|t_{1}{\mathbf{w}}-t_{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\|_{2}^{2}.

The first and second inequalities follow from Lemma
24
and Lemma
23
, respectively. For the sake of brevity, let
{\mathbf{u}}_{1}(t)=\bm{\psi}(t,{\delta}_{1}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}),{\mathbf{u}}_{2}(t)=\bm{\psi}(t,\delta_{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{*})\text{ and }{\mathbf{s}}_{1}(t)=\frac{1}{\delta_{1}}{\mathbf{u}}_{1}\left(\frac{t}{\delta_{1}^{L-2}}\right),{\mathbf{s}}_{2}(t)=\frac{1}{\delta_{2}}{\mathbf{u}}_{2}\left(\frac{t}{\delta_{2}^{L-2}}\right),
where recall that
\delta_{2}\geq\delta_{1}>0
. Also, let

{\mathbf{z}}(t)=\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}}{(1-tL(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*}))^{\frac{1}{L-2}}},

which is the solution of

\dot{{\mathbf{z}}}=\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{z}}),{\mathbf{z}}(0)={\mathbf{w}}_{*}.

Now,
{\mathbf{s}}_{1}(0)={\mathbf{w}}_{*}
. Define

T^{*}_{1}=\min_{t\geq 0}\left\{t:\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{s}}_{1}(t)}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{1}(t)\|_{2}}=1-\gamma\right\}.

Then, following a a similar way as in the proof of Lemma
31
to get
eq.

73
,
eq.

74
, and
eq.

72
, we can show that there exists a
C_{1},C_{2}>0
such that, for all
t\in[0,\frac{\left(1-\delta_{1}^{L-2}C_{1}\right)}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}]
,

\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{1}(t)-{\mathbf{z}}(t)\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{C_{2}\delta_{1}^{L}}{\left(1-tL(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})\right)^{\frac{L+1}{L-2}}},\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{s}}_{1}(t)}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{1}(t)\|_{2}}\geq 1-\gamma,\text{ and }

(76)

\|{\mathbf{s}}_{1}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}\leq\frac{1}{1-tL(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},

for all sufficiently small
\delta_{1}>0
. Now, suppose
\delta_{2}^{L-2}\leq\frac{1}{C_{1}}
, then

T_{2}^{*}\coloneqq\frac{1}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}\left(1-\frac{\delta_{1}^{L-2}}{\delta_{2}^{L-2}}\right)\leq\frac{\left(1-\delta_{1}^{L-2}C_{1}\right)}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}.

Next, note that

\delta_{1}{\mathbf{z}}\left(T_{2}^{*}\right)=\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}}{(1-T_{2}^{*}L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*}))^{\frac{1}{L-2}}}=\frac{\delta_{1}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}}{(1-(1-\delta_{1}^{L-2}/\delta_{2}^{L-2}))^{\frac{1}{L-2}}}=\delta_{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{*},\text{ and }

\|{\mathbf{s}}_{1}(T_{2}^{*})-{\mathbf{z}}(T_{2}^{*})\|_{2}\leq\left(\frac{C_{2}\delta_{1}^{L}}{\left(1-T_{2}^{*}L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})\right)^{\frac{L+1}{L-2}}}\right)=\frac{C_{2}\delta_{2}^{L+1}}{\delta_{1}}.

Hence,

\left\|\bm{\psi}\left(\frac{{1}/{\delta_{1}^{L-2}}-{1}/{\delta_{2}^{L-2}}}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},\delta_{1}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right)-\delta_{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right\|_{2}=\|\delta_{1}{\mathbf{s}}_{1}(T_{2}^{*})-\delta_{1}{\mathbf{z}}(T_{2}^{*})\|_{2}\leq C_{2}\delta_{2}^{L+1}.

(77)

Now, note that

\bm{\psi}\left(t+\frac{{1}/{\delta_{1}^{L-2}}}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},\delta_{1}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right)=\bm{\psi}\left(t+\frac{{1}/{\delta_{2}^{L-2}}}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},\bm{\psi}\left(\frac{{1}/{\delta_{1}^{L-2}}-{1}/{\delta_{2}^{L-2}}}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},\delta_{1}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right)\right).

Combining the above equality with
eq.

77
and Lemma
31
, we get that for any fixed
t\in(-\infty,\infty)
, there exist a constant
C>0
such that

\left\|\bm{\psi}\left(t+\frac{{1}/{\delta_{1}^{L-2}}}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},\delta_{1}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right)-\bm{\psi}\left(t+\frac{{1}/{\delta_{2}^{L-2}}}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},\delta_{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right)\right\|_{2}\leq C\delta_{2},

which implies
{\mathbf{p}}(t)
exists for all
t\in(-\infty,\infty)
.

We next prove that
\mathcal{L}({\mathbf{p}}(0))\leq\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{0})-\eta
, for some
\eta>0
. Let
{\mathbf{u}}(t)=\bm{\psi}(t,\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{*})
and
{\mathbf{z}}(t)={{\mathbf{w}}_{*}}/{(1-tL(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*}))^{\frac{1}{L-2}}}.
From
eq.

76
, there exist constants
B_{1},B_{2}
such that

\left\|\frac{{\mathbf{u}}(t/\delta^{L-2})}{\delta}-{\mathbf{z}}(t)\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{B_{2}\delta^{L}}{\left(1-tL(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})\right)^{\frac{L+1}{L-2}}},\text{ for all }t\in\left[0,\frac{\left(1-B_{1}\delta^{L-2}\right)}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}\right].

(78)

Define
\alpha\coloneqq\max_{\|{\mathbf{w}}\|_{2}=1}\|\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{X}},{\mathbf{w}})\|_{2}
. Similar to
eq.

51
, we have

\mathcal{L}({\mathbf{w}})\leq\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{0})+Kn\|\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{X}},{\mathbf{w}})\|_{2}^{2}-\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}})\leq\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{0})+\alpha^{2}Kn\|{\mathbf{w}}\|_{2}^{2L}-\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}).

(79)

Now, choose
\epsilon>0
sufficiently small such that

\epsilon\leq\frac{1}{B_{1}},\text{ and }\alpha^{2}Kn({\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2(L-2)}}+B_{1}\epsilon^{\frac{L+1/2}{L-2}})^{2L}\leq\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}})-\frac{\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}{2},\text{ if }\|{\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\|_{2}\leq\epsilon^{\frac{L}{L-2}}B_{1},

where the second inequality holds true since
\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}})
is continuous. Note that

\frac{\left(1-B_{1}\delta^{L-2}\right)}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}\geq\frac{\left(1-\delta^{L-2}/\epsilon\right)}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}\coloneqq T_{2}.

Hence, using
eq.

78
,
\|{\mathbf{u}}(T_{2}/\delta^{L-2})\|_{2}\leq\epsilon^{1/(L-2)}+B_{2}\epsilon^{(L+1)/(L-2)}
. Also, if we define
\tau\coloneqq{\mathbf{u}}(T_{2}/\delta^{L-2})-\delta{\mathbf{z}}(T_{2}/\delta^{L-2})
, then,
\|\tau\|_{2}\leq B_{2}\epsilon^{(L+1)/(L-2)}
. Therefore, using
eq.

79
, we get

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}\left({\mathbf{u}}\left(\frac{T_{2}}{\delta^{L-2}}\right)\right)\leq\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{0})+\alpha^{2}Kn\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\left(\frac{T_{2}}{\delta^{L-2}}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2L}-\mathcal{N}\left({\mathbf{u}}\left(\frac{T_{2}}{\delta^{L-2}}\right)\right),

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{0})+\alpha^{2}Kn(\epsilon^{1/(L-2)}+B_{2}\epsilon^{(L+1)/(L-2)})^{2L}-\mathcal{N}\left({\mathbf{u}}\left(\frac{T_{2}}{\delta^{L-2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{0})+\epsilon^{\frac{L}{L-2}}\left(\alpha^{2}Kn({\epsilon}^{\frac{1}{2(L-2)}}+B_{1}\epsilon^{\frac{L+1/2}{L-2}})^{2L}-\mathcal{N}\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon^{\frac{1}{L-2}}}{\mathbf{u}}\left(\frac{T_{2}}{\delta^{L-2}}\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{0})-\epsilon^{\frac{L}{L-2}}\frac{\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}{2},

where the last inequality follows from the choice of
\epsilon
and since
\|{\mathbf{u}}(T_{2}/\delta^{L-2})/\epsilon^{1/(L-2)}-{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\|_{2}=\tau/\epsilon^{1/(L-2)}\leq B_{1}\epsilon^{L/(L-2)}.
Let
\eta=\epsilon^{L/(L-2)}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})/2
, then, from the above equation,

\mathcal{L}\left(\bm{\psi}\left(\frac{\left(1/\delta^{L-2}-\epsilon\right)}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right)\right)\leq\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{0})-\eta,

where note that
\epsilon>0
and fixed, and thus,
\eta
is fixed. Since the loss decreases with time, the above equation implies

\mathcal{L}\left(\bm{\psi}\left(\frac{1/\delta^{L-2}}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right)\right)\leq\mathcal{L}\left(\bm{\psi}\left(\frac{\left(1/\delta^{L-2}-\epsilon\right)}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right)\right)\leq\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{0})-\eta.

Taking
\delta\to 0
, gives us
\mathcal{L}({\mathbf{p}}(0))\leq\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{0})-\eta,
which completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 33
.

Consider the setting in
Theorem

11
. There exists
T_{1},a_{1}\geq 0
such that for all sufficiently small
\delta>0
,

\left\|\bm{\psi}\left(\frac{T_{1}}{\delta^{L-2}}+\frac{T}{\widetilde{\delta}^{L-2}}\left(1-\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{2(L-2)L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}}\right),\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)-b_{\delta}\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{\Delta}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right\|_{2}\leq a_{1}\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{(L+1)\Delta}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}},

where
\widetilde{\delta}\in(A_{2}\delta-A_{1}\delta^{L+1},A_{2}\delta+A_{1}\delta^{L+1})
, for some positive constants
A_{2},A_{1}
. Also,
T\geq\frac{1}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}
, and
b_{\delta}^{L-2}\in\left[\frac{1}{TL(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},1\right]
depends on
\delta
.

Proof.

Choose
\alpha\in(0,1)
sufficiently small such that

0<\frac{(L(L-1)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\alpha)(1+\alpha)^{L-2}}{L(L-2)(\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})-\alpha)}+1<\frac{2L-1}{L-2}.

(80)

Next, we choose
\gamma>0
sufficiently small such that for all unit-norm vector
{\mathbf{w}}
satisfying
{\mathbf{w}}^{\top}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\geq 1-\gamma
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})-\alpha\leq\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}})\leq\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*}),\|\nabla^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}})\|_{2}\leq L(L-1)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\alpha,

(81)

\displaystyle{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}})-L\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}){\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{w}}-\frac{\Delta}{2}\|{\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\|_{2}^{2}\geq 0,

(82)

and for all
t_{2}\geq t_{1}\geq 0
,

\left(\nabla\mathcal{N}(t_{1}{\mathbf{w}})-\nabla\mathcal{N}(t_{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{*})\right)^{\top}\left(t_{1}{\mathbf{w}}-t_{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right)\leq L(L-1)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})t_{2}^{L-2}\|t_{1}{\mathbf{w}}-t_{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\|_{2}^{2}.

(83)

The first and second inequalities follow from Lemma
24
and Lemma
21
. The third and fourth inequalities follow from Lemma
24
and Lemma
23
. Define
f({\mathbf{w}})=\mathcal{J}({\mathbf{X}};{\mathbf{w}})^{\top}(\ell^{\prime}(\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{X}};{\mathbf{w}}),{\mathbf{y}})-\ell^{\prime}(\mathbf{0},{\mathbf{y}}))
, then, as shown in the proof of Lemma
31
, there exists a
\beta>0
such that

\|f({\mathbf{w}})\|_{2}\leq\beta\|{\mathbf{w}}\|_{2}^{2L-1},\text{ for all }{\mathbf{w}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}.

(84)

Let
{\mathbf{z}}_{1}(t)
denote the solution of

\dot{{\mathbf{z}}}=\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{z}}),{\mathbf{z}}(0)={\mathbf{w}}_{0}.

(85)

Since
{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\in\mathcal{S}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})
,
{\mathbf{z}}_{1}(t)
converges to
{\mathbf{w}}_{*}
in direction. Thus, we can assume there exists some time
T_{\gamma}
and a constant
B_{1}
such that

\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{z}}_{1}(T_{\gamma})}{\|{\mathbf{z}}_{1}(T_{\gamma})\|_{2}}=1-\gamma^{2}/8\text{ and }\|{\mathbf{z}}_{1}(t)\|_{2}\leq B_{1},\text{ for all }t\in[0,T_{\gamma}].

Define
{\mathbf{w}}(t)=\bm{\psi}(t,\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0})\text{ and }{\mathbf{s}}_{w}(t)=\frac{1}{\delta}{\mathbf{w}}\left(\frac{t}{\delta^{L-2}}\right),
then
{\mathbf{s}}_{w}(t)
is the solution of

\dot{{\mathbf{s}}}=\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{s}})-\mathcal{J}({\mathbf{X}};{\mathbf{s}})^{\top}(\ell^{\prime}(\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{X}};\delta{\mathbf{s}}),{\mathbf{y}})-\ell^{\prime}(\mathbf{0},{\mathbf{y}}))=\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{s}})-f(\delta{\mathbf{s}})/\delta^{L-1},{\mathbf{s}}(0)={\mathbf{w}}_{0}.

Note that
\|{\mathbf{s}}_{w}(0)-{\mathbf{z}}_{1}(0)\|_{2}=0
. Define

T_{1}^{*}=\min_{t\geq 0}\{t:\|{\mathbf{s}}_{w}(t)-{\mathbf{z}}_{1}(t)\|_{2}=\gamma^{2}/4\}.

Then, for all
t\in[0,T_{1}^{*}]
,
\|{\mathbf{s}}_{w}(t)-{\mathbf{z}}_{1}(t)\|_{2}\leq\gamma^{2}/4
. Next, we show that for all sufficiently small
\delta>0
,
T_{1}^{*}>T_{\gamma}
. For the sake of contradiction, suppose
T_{1}^{*}\leq T_{\gamma}
. Let
B_{2}=B_{1}+\gamma^{2}/4
, then
\|{\mathbf{s}}_{w}(t)\|_{2}\leq B_{2}
, for all
t\in[0,T_{1}^{*}]
. Since
\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{s}})
is locally Lipschitz, there exists a
\mu_{1}>0
such that

\|\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{s}}_{w}(t))-\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{z}}_{1}(t))\|_{2}\leq\mu_{1}\|{\mathbf{s}}_{w}(t)-{\mathbf{z}}_{1}(t)\|_{2},\text{ for all }t\in[0,T_{1}^{*}].

Hence, for all
t\in[0,T_{1}^{*}]
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{w}(t)-{\mathbf{z}}_{1}(t)\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=({\mathbf{s}}_{w}-{\mathbf{z}}_{1})^{\top}(\dot{{\mathbf{s}}}_{w}-\dot{{\mathbf{z}}}_{1})

\displaystyle=({\mathbf{s}}_{w}-{\mathbf{z}}_{1})^{\top}(\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{s}}_{w})-\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{z}}_{1}))-({\mathbf{s}}_{w}-{\mathbf{z}}_{1})^{\top}f(\delta{\mathbf{s}}_{w})/\delta^{L-1}

\displaystyle\leq\mu_{1}\|{\mathbf{s}}_{w}-{\mathbf{z}}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+\beta\delta^{L}\|{\mathbf{s}}_{w}-{\mathbf{z}}_{1}\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{s}}_{w}\|_{2}^{2L-1}

\displaystyle\leq\mu_{1}\|{\mathbf{s}}_{w}-{\mathbf{z}}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+\beta\delta^{L}B_{2}^{2L-1}\|{\mathbf{s}}_{w}-{\mathbf{z}}_{1}\|_{2},

which implies

\frac{d}{dt}\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{w}(t)-{\mathbf{z}}_{1}(t)\right\|_{2}\leq\mu_{1}\|{\mathbf{s}}_{w}-{\mathbf{z}}_{1}\|_{2}+\beta\delta^{L}B_{2}^{2L-1}.

Hence, using Lemma
30
, we get

\|{\mathbf{s}}_{w}(t)-{\mathbf{z}}_{1}(t)\|_{2}\leq\beta\delta^{L}B_{2}^{2L-1}e^{\mu_{1}t}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\mu_{1}s}ds\leq B_{3}\delta^{L}e^{\mu_{1}t},

where
B_{3}
is a sufficiently large constant. From the definition of
T_{1}^{*}
, we get

\gamma^{2}/4=\|{\mathbf{s}}_{w}(T_{1}^{*})-{\mathbf{z}}_{1}(T_{1}^{*})\|_{2}\leq B_{3}\delta^{L}e^{\mu_{1}T_{1}^{*}}\leq B_{3}\delta^{L}e^{\mu_{1}T_{\gamma}},

where the second inequality uses
T_{1}^{*}\leq T_{\gamma}
. Clearly, the above inequality is not possible if
\delta
is sufficiently small, leading to a contradiction. Hence,
T_{1}^{*}>T_{\gamma}
. Let
A_{1}=B_{3}e^{\mu_{1}T_{\gamma}}
, then

\|{\mathbf{s}}_{w}(T_{\gamma})-{\mathbf{z}}_{1}(T_{\gamma})\|_{2}\leq A_{1}\delta^{L}.

(86)

Next, let
\tau_{1}(t)={\mathbf{s}}_{w}(t)-{\mathbf{z}}_{1}(t)
, then, for all sufficiently small
\delta>0
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{s}}_{w}(T_{\gamma})}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{w}(T_{\gamma})\|_{2}}=\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{z}}_{1}(T_{\gamma})+{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}\tau_{1}(T_{\gamma})}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{w}(T_{\gamma})\|_{2}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{(1-\gamma^{2}/8)\|{\mathbf{z}}_{1}(T_{\gamma})\|_{2}-A_{1}\delta^{L}}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{w}(T_{\gamma})\|_{2}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{(1-\gamma^{2}/8)\|{\mathbf{z}}_{1}(T_{\gamma})\|_{2}-A_{1}\delta^{L}}{\|{\mathbf{z}}_{1}(T_{\gamma})\|_{2}+A_{1}\delta^{L}}\geq 1-\gamma^{2}/4.

Let
A_{2}=\|{\mathbf{z}}_{1}(T_{\gamma})\|_{2},
where note that
A_{2}
is a constant that does not depend on
\delta
. Define
{\mathbf{w}}_{1}={\mathbf{s}}_{w}(T_{\gamma})/\|{\mathbf{s}}_{w}(T_{\gamma})\|_{2}
and
\widetilde{\delta}=\delta\|{\mathbf{s}}_{w}(T_{\gamma})\|_{2}
. From
eq.

86
, we have

\widetilde{\delta}\in(A_{2}\delta-A_{1}\delta^{L+1},A_{2}\delta+A_{1}\delta^{L+1}),

thus,
\widetilde{\delta}
can be made sufficiently small by choosing
\delta
sufficiently small. Next, let
{\mathbf{u}}(t)=\bm{\psi}(t+T_{\gamma}/\delta^{L-2},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0})
. Then,

{\mathbf{u}}(t)=\bm{\psi}\left(t+\frac{T_{\gamma}}{\delta^{L-2}},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)=\bm{\psi}\left(t,\bm{\psi}\left(\frac{T_{\gamma}}{\delta^{L-2}},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)\right)=\bm{\psi}\left(t,\delta\|{\mathbf{s}}_{w}(T_{\gamma})\|_{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{1}\right)=\bm{\psi}\left(t,\widetilde{\delta}{\mathbf{w}}_{1}\right).

Define
{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)=\frac{1}{\widetilde{\delta}}{\mathbf{u}}\left(\frac{t}{\widetilde{\delta}^{L-2}}\right),
then
{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)
is the solution of

\dot{{\mathbf{s}}}=\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{s}})-\mathcal{J}({\mathbf{X}};{\mathbf{s}})^{\top}(\ell^{\prime}(\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{X}};\widetilde{\delta}{\mathbf{s}}),{\mathbf{y}})-\ell^{\prime}(\mathbf{0},{\mathbf{y}}))=\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{s}})-f(\widetilde{\delta}{\mathbf{s}})/\widetilde{\delta}^{L-1},{\mathbf{s}}(0)={\mathbf{w}}_{1}.

Define
{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)
to be the solution of

\dot{{\mathbf{z}}}=\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{z}}),{\mathbf{z}}(0)={\mathbf{w}}_{1}.

(87)

Since
{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(0)^{\top}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\geq 1-\gamma^{2}/4>1-\gamma
, we may assume without loss of generality that
\gamma>0
is sufficiently small such that Lemma
26
is applicable. Hence, there exists a
T>0
such that

\left(1-\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)}{\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)\|_{2}}\right)\leq\gamma\left(1-\frac{t}{T}\right)^{\frac{\Delta}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}}\text{ and }\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)\|_{2}=\frac{\|{\mathbf{g}}(t)\|_{2}}{(T-t)^{\frac{1}{L-2}}},

(88)

for all
t\in[0,T)
, where
\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)\|
is an increasing function and
\|{\mathbf{g}}(t)\|_{2}
is a decreasing function in
[0,T)
, and
\|{\mathbf{g}}(0)\|_{2}^{L-2}=T,\|{\mathbf{g}}(T)\|_{2}^{L-2}=1/(L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})).
Also, from Lemma
26
and
eq.

81
, we have

T\leq\frac{1}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{1})}\leq\frac{1}{L(L-2)(\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})-\alpha)}.

(89)

Next, define
\eta=\frac{2(L-2)L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}
and
\overline{T}=T(1-\widetilde{\delta}^{\eta})
, then

\left(1-\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(\overline{T})}{\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(\overline{T})\|_{2}}\right)\leq{\gamma\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{2L\Delta}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}}},\text{ and }\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(\overline{T})\|_{2}^{L-2}=\frac{\|{\mathbf{g}}(\overline{T})\|_{2}^{L-2}}{T\widetilde{\delta}^{\eta}}.

Therefore,

\left\|\frac{{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(\overline{T})}{\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(\overline{T})\|_{2}}-{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}=2\left(1-\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(\overline{T})}{\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(\overline{T})\|_{2}}\right)\leq{2\gamma\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{2L\Delta}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}}}.

(90)

Define
\mu_{2}=(L(L-1)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\alpha)(1+\alpha)^{L-2}
, and let

C_{1}=\frac{2\beta(1+\alpha)^{2L-1}}{\left(\frac{L+1}{L-2}-\mu_{2}\|{\mathbf{g}}(T)\|_{2}^{L-2}\right)}\frac{\|{\mathbf{g}}(0)\|_{2}^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}}{\|{\mathbf{g}}(T)\|_{2}^{\frac{L+1}{L-2}}}>0,

where the inequality holds since, from
eq.

80
,
\frac{L+1}{L-2}-\mu_{2}\|{\mathbf{g}}(T)\|_{2}^{L-2}>0
. Now, since
{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(0)/\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(0)\|_{2}\geq 1-\gamma^{2}/4
and
0=\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}({0})-{\mathbf{z}}_{2}({0})\right\|_{2}<C_{1}\widetilde{\delta}^{L}\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}({0})\|_{2}^{L+1}
, we define

T_{2}^{*}=\min_{t\geq 0}\left\{t:\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)\|_{2}}=1-\gamma\right\},T_{3}^{*}=\min_{t\geq 0}\{t:\left\|{\mathbf{s}}({t})-{\mathbf{z}}_{2}({t})\right\|_{2}=C_{1}\widetilde{\delta}^{L}\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}({t})\|_{2}^{L+1}\},

and
T_{4}^{*}=\min(T_{2}^{*},T_{3}^{*}).

Thus, for all
t\in[0,{T}_{4}^{*}]
, we have

\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}({t})-{\mathbf{z}}_{2}({t})\right\|_{2}\leq C_{1}\widetilde{\delta}^{L}\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}({t})\|_{2}^{L+1},\text{ and }\frac{{\mathbf{s}}_{u}({t})^{\top}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)\|_{2}}\geq 1-\gamma.

We next show that
\overline{T}\leq T_{4}^{*}
, for all sufficiently small
\delta>0
. For the sake of contradiction, let
\overline{T}>T_{4}^{*}
. Now, since
\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)\|_{2}
increases with time, for all
t\in[0,T_{4}^{*}]
, we have

C_{1}\widetilde{\delta}^{L}\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}({t})\|_{2}^{L}\leq C_{1}\widetilde{\delta}^{L}\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(\overline{T})\|_{2}^{L}\leq\frac{C_{1}\|{\mathbf{g}}(\overline{T})\|_{2}^{L}}{T^{\frac{L}{L-2}}}\widetilde{\delta}^{L-\frac{L\eta}{(L-2)}}=\frac{C_{1}\|{\mathbf{g}}(\overline{T})\|_{2}^{L}}{T^{\frac{L}{L-2}}}\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{L\Delta}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}}\leq\alpha,

for all sufficiently small
\delta>0
. Since
\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}({t})-{\mathbf{z}}_{2}({t})\right\|_{2}\leq C_{1}\widetilde{\delta}^{L}\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}({t})\|_{2}^{L+1}
, we get

\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}({t})-{\mathbf{z}}_{2}({t})\right\|_{2}\leq\alpha\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}({t})\|_{2},\text{ for all }t\in[0,T_{4}^{*}],

which implies

0<(1-\alpha)\left\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}({t})\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}({t})\right\|_{2}\leq(1+\alpha)\left\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}({t})\right\|_{2}.

(91)

Then, for all
t\in[0,T_{4}^{*}]
,

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)\|_{2}}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}\left({\mathbf{I}}-\frac{{\mathbf{s}}_{u}{\mathbf{s}}_{u}^{\top}}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)\frac{\dot{{\mathbf{s}}}_{u}}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}\|_{2}}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}\left({\mathbf{I}}-\frac{{\mathbf{s}}_{u}{\mathbf{s}}_{u}^{\top}}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)\frac{\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{s}}_{u})}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}\|_{2}}-{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}\left({\mathbf{I}}-\frac{{\mathbf{s}}_{u}{\mathbf{s}}_{u}^{\top}}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)\frac{f(\widetilde{\delta}{\mathbf{s}}_{u})}{\widetilde{\delta}^{L-1}\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}\|_{2}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{s}}_{u})}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}\|_{2}}-\frac{L{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{s}}_{u}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{s}}_{u})}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}\|_{2}^{3}}-\beta\widetilde{\delta}^{L}\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}\|_{2}^{2L-2}

\displaystyle=\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}\|_{2}^{L-2}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}\nabla\mathcal{N}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{s}}_{u}}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}\|_{2}}\right)-L{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{s}}_{u}}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}\|_{2}}\right)\mathcal{N}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{s}}_{u}}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}\|_{2}}\right)\right)-\beta\widetilde{\delta}^{L}\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}\|_{2}^{2L-2}

\displaystyle\geq\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}\|_{2}^{L-2}\frac{\Delta}{2}\left\|\frac{{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)\|_{2}}-{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\beta\widetilde{\delta}^{L}\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}\|_{2}^{2L-2}

\displaystyle\geq-\beta\widetilde{\delta}^{L}\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}\|_{2}^{2L-2}\geq-\beta(1+\alpha)^{2L-2}\widetilde{\delta}^{L}\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)\|_{2}^{2L-2},

where the second inequality uses
eq.

82
, and the last inequality uses
eq.

91
. Hence,

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)\|_{2}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(0)}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(0)\|_{2}}-\beta\widetilde{\delta}^{L}(1+\alpha)^{2L-2}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{\|{\mathbf{g}}(s)\|_{2}^{2L-2}ds}{(T-s)^{\frac{2L-2}{L-2}}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(0)}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(0)\|_{2}}-\beta\|{\mathbf{g}}(0)\|_{2}^{2L-2}\widetilde{\delta}^{L}(1+\alpha)^{2L-2}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{ds}{(T-s)^{\frac{2L-2}{L-2}}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(0)}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(0)\|_{2}}-\frac{\beta(L-2)\|{\mathbf{g}}(0)\|_{2}^{2L-2}\widetilde{\delta}^{L}(1+\alpha)^{2L-2}}{L(T-t)^{\frac{L}{L-2}}},

where the second inequality holds since
{\mathbf{g}}(t)
is a decreasing function. Since
\overline{T}>T_{4}^{*}
, we get

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(T_{4}^{*})}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(T_{4}^{*})\|_{2}}-\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(0)}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(0)\|_{2}}

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{(L-2)\beta\|{\mathbf{g}}(0)\|_{2}^{2L-2}\widetilde{\delta}^{L}(1+\alpha)^{2L-2}}{L(T-\overline{T})^{\frac{L}{L-2}}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{(L-2)\beta\|{\mathbf{g}}(0)\|_{2}^{2L-2}\widetilde{\delta}^{L}(1+\alpha)^{2L-2}}{LT^{\frac{L}{L-2}}\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{\eta L}{L-2}}}.

(92)

Next, note that for
t\in[0,T_{4}^{*}]
, using the mean value theorem, we have

\left\|\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t))-\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t))\right\|_{2}\leq\|\nabla^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{r}})\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)-{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)\right\|_{2},

where
{\mathbf{r}}=(1-\lambda){\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)+\lambda{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)
, for some
\lambda\in(0,1)
. Since
\nabla^{2}\mathcal{N}(\cdot)
is
(L-2)
-homogeneous,

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{r}})\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\|\nabla^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{r}}/\|{\mathbf{r}}\|_{2})\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{r}}\|_{2}^{L-2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{r}}/\|{\mathbf{r}}\|_{2})\|_{2}\max(\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2},\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2})

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{r}}/\|{\mathbf{r}}\|_{2})\|_{2}(1+\alpha)^{L-2}\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}.

From
eq.

81
, we know
\|\nabla^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{r}}/\|{\mathbf{r}}\|_{2})\|_{2}\leq L(L-1)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\alpha
. Thus, for all
t\in[0,T_{4}^{*}]
,

\left\|\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t))-\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t))\right\|_{2}\leq(L(L-1)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\alpha)(1+\alpha)^{L-2}\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)-{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)\right\|_{2}.

Recall that
\mu_{2}=(L(L-1)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\alpha)(1+\alpha)^{L-2}
, then

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\left\|{{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)}-{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left({{\mathbf{s}}_{u}}-{\mathbf{z}}_{2}\right)^{\top}\left(\dot{{\mathbf{s}}}_{u}-\dot{{\mathbf{z}}}_{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\left({{\mathbf{s}}_{u}}{}-{\mathbf{z}}_{2}\right)^{\top}\left(\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{s}}_{u})-\nabla\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{z}}_{2})\right)-\left({{\mathbf{s}}_{u}}{}-{\mathbf{z}}_{2}\right)^{\top}f(\widetilde{\delta}{\mathbf{s}}_{u})/\widetilde{\delta}^{L-1}

\displaystyle\leq\mu_{2}\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}\left\|{{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)}{}-{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)\right\|_{2}^{2}+\beta\widetilde{\delta}^{L}\left\|{{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)}-{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)\right\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)\|_{2}^{2L-1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu_{2}\|{\mathbf{g}}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}}{(T-t)}

\displaystyle\left\|{{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)}{}-{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)\right\|_{2}^{2}+\beta(1+\alpha)^{2L-1}\widetilde{\delta}^{L}\left\|{{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)}{}-{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)\right\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)\|_{2}^{2L-1}.

Define
\beta_{1}=\beta(1+\alpha)^{2L-1}
, then, from the above equation, we have

\frac{d}{dt}\left\|{{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)}{}-{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{\mu_{2}\|{\mathbf{g}}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}}{(T-t)}\left\|{{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)}{}-{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)\right\|_{2}+\beta_{1}\widetilde{\delta}^{L}\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)\|_{2}^{2L-1}.

Using Lemma
30
, we get

\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)-{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{\beta_{1}\widetilde{\delta}^{L}}{P(t)}\int_{0}^{t}P(s)\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)\|_{2}^{2L-1}ds,

where
P(t)=e^{-\int_{0}^{t}b(s)ds}
and
b(t)=\frac{\mu_{2}\|{\mathbf{g}}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}}{(T-t)}
. The above equation implies that

\displaystyle\frac{\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(t)-{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)\right\|_{2}}{\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(t)\|_{2}^{L+1}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\beta_{1}\widetilde{\delta}^{L}}{P(t)\|{\mathbf{z}}(t)\|_{2}^{L+1}}\int_{0}^{t}P(s)\|{\mathbf{z}}(t)\|_{2}^{2L-1}ds

\displaystyle={\beta_{1}\widetilde{\delta}^{L}}\frac{\int_{0}^{t}P(s)\|{\mathbf{g}}(s)\|_{2}^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}/(T-t)^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}}{P(t)\|{\mathbf{g}}(t)\|_{2}^{\frac{L+1}{L-2}}/(T-t)^{\frac{L+1}{L-2}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\beta_{1}\|{\mathbf{g}}(0)\|_{2}^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}\widetilde{\delta}^{L}}{\|{\mathbf{g}}(T)\|_{2}^{\frac{L+1}{L-2}}}\frac{\int_{0}^{t}P(s)/(T-t)^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}}{P(t)/(T-t)^{\frac{L+1}{L-2}}},

where in the last inequality we used
\|{\mathbf{g}}(T)\|_{2}\leq\|{\mathbf{g}}(s)\|_{2}\leq\|{\mathbf{g}}(0)\|_{2}
, for all
s\in[0,T)
. Let

h(t)\coloneqq\frac{\int_{0}^{t}P(s)/(T-s)^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}}{P(t)/(T-t)^{\frac{L+1}{L-2}}},\text{ for all }t\in[0,T).

We will later show that
h(t)
is an increasing function in
[0,T)
and

\lim_{t\rightarrow T}h(t)=\frac{1}{\left(\frac{L+1}{L-2}-\mu_{2}\|{\mathbf{g}}(T)\|_{2}^{L-2}\right)}.

Assuming the above statement is true and since
T>\overline{T}>T_{4}^{*}
, we have

\frac{\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(T_{4}^{*})-{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(T_{4}^{*})\right\|_{2}}{\beta_{1}\widetilde{\delta}^{L}\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(T_{4}^{*})\|_{2}^{L+1}}\leq\frac{h(T_{4}^{*})\|{\mathbf{g}}(0)\|_{2}^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}}{\|{\mathbf{g}}(T)\|_{2}^{\frac{L+1}{L-2}}}\leq\frac{h(T)\|{\mathbf{g}}(0)\|_{2}^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}}{\|{\mathbf{g}}(T)\|_{2}^{\frac{L+1}{L-2}}}=\frac{C_{1}}{2\beta_{1}}.

(93)

Now, by definition of
T_{4}^{*}
, either

\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(T_{4}^{*})}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(T_{4}^{*})\|_{2}}=1-\gamma\text{ or }\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}({T_{4}^{*}})-{\mathbf{z}}_{2}({T_{4}^{*}})\right\|_{2}=C_{1}\widetilde{\delta}^{L}\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}({T_{4}^{*}})\|_{2}^{L+1}.

However, from
eq.

92
,

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(T_{4}^{*})}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(T_{4}^{*})\|_{2}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{*}^{\top}{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(0)}{\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(0)\|_{2}}-\frac{(L-2)\beta\|{\mathbf{g}}(0)\|_{2}^{2L-2}\widetilde{\delta}^{L}(1+\alpha)^{2L-2}}{LT^{\frac{L}{L-2}}\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{\eta L}{L-2}}}

\displaystyle\geq 1-\gamma^{2}/4-\frac{(L-2)\beta\|{\mathbf{g}}(0)\|_{2}^{2L-2}\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{L\Delta}{L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}}(1+\alpha)^{2L-2}}{LT^{\frac{L}{L-2}}}>1-\gamma/2,

for all sufficiently small
\delta>0
. Also, from
eq.

93
, we have

C_{1}\widetilde{\delta}^{L}=\frac{\left\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(T_{4}^{*})-{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(T_{4}^{*})\right\|_{2}}{\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(T_{4}^{*})\|_{2}^{L+1}}\leq C_{1}\widetilde{\delta}^{L}/2.

Hence, there is a contradiction, which implies
T_{4}^{*}\geq\overline{T}
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{u}}(\overline{T}/\widetilde{\delta}^{L-2})-\widetilde{\delta}\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(\overline{T})\|_{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\|\widetilde{\delta}{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(\overline{T})-\widetilde{\delta}\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(\overline{T})\|_{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{\delta}\|{\mathbf{s}}_{u}(\overline{T})-{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(\overline{T})\|_{2}+\widetilde{\delta}\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(\overline{T})-\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(\overline{T})\|_{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\widetilde{\delta}^{L+1}\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(\overline{T})\|_{2}^{L+1}+\widetilde{\delta}\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(\overline{T})\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(\overline{T})/\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(\overline{T})\|_{2}-{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}\widetilde{\delta}^{L+1}\|{\mathbf{g}}(\overline{T})\|_{2}^{L+1}}{T^{\frac{L+1}{L-2}}\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{\eta(L+1)}{L-2}}}+\frac{\sqrt{2\gamma}\|{\mathbf{g}}(\overline{T})\|_{2}\widetilde{\delta}\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{L\Delta}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}}}{T^{\frac{1}{L-2}}\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{\eta}{L-2}}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{2}\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{(L+1)\Delta}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}}+C_{3}\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{(L+1)\Delta}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}},

for some constants
C_{2},C_{3}
and for all sufficiently small
\delta>0
, where the third inequality follows from
eq.

88
and
eq.

90
. Also, note that

\widetilde{\delta}\|{\mathbf{z}}_{2}(\overline{T})\|_{2}=\widetilde{\delta}{\|{\mathbf{g}}(\overline{T})\|_{2}}/{(T^{\frac{1}{L-2}}\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{\eta}{L-2}})}=\|{\mathbf{g}}(\overline{T})\|_{2}\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{\Delta}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}}/T^{\frac{1}{L-2}}.

Thus, using the above two equations and the definition of
{\mathbf{u}}(t)
, we get

\left\|\bm{\psi}({T}_{\gamma}/\delta^{L-2}+\overline{T}/\widetilde{\delta}^{L-2},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0})-\|{\mathbf{g}}(\overline{T})\|_{2}\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{\Delta}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}}/T^{\frac{1}{L-2}}\right\|_{2}\leq a_{1}\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{(L+1)\Delta}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}},

where
a_{1}
is a sufficiently large constant. Note that, since
\overline{T}
depends on
\delta
,
\|{\mathbf{g}}(\overline{T})\|_{2}
may also depend on
\delta
, but
\|{\mathbf{g}}(\overline{T})\|_{2}^{L-2}\in[1/(L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})),T]
.

We next prove
h(t)
is an increasing function by showing
h^{\prime}(t)\geq 0
for
t\in[0,T)
. Note that

P^{\prime}(t)=-\mu_{2}\|{\mathbf{g}}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}P(t)/(T-t).

From the quotient rule of differentiation, the denominator of
h^{\prime}(t)
is
P^{2}(t)/(T-t)^{\frac{2L+2}{L-2}}
, and the numerator can be written as

\displaystyle\frac{P(t)}{(T-t)^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}}\frac{P(t)}{(T-t)^{\frac{L+1}{L-2}}}-\left(\int_{0}^{t}\frac{P(s)}{(T-s)^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}}ds\right)\left(\frac{-\mu_{2}\|{\mathbf{g}}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}P(t)}{(T-t)^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}}+\frac{L+1}{L-2}\frac{P(t)}{(T-t)^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{P^{2}(t)}{(T-t)^{\frac{3L}{L-2}}}\left(1-\left(\frac{L+1}{L-2}-\mu_{2}\|{\mathbf{g}}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}\right)h(t)\right).

Let
r(t)={\left(\frac{L+1}{L-2}-\mu_{2}\|{\mathbf{g}}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}\right)}
, then
h^{\prime}(t)=\frac{1-r(t)h(t)}{(T-t)},
and
r(t)
is an increasing function since
\|{\mathbf{g}}(t)\|_{2}
is a decreasing function. Next, note that

1-r(t)h(t)=\frac{{P(t)}/{(T-t)^{\frac{L+1}{L-2}}}-r(t)\int_{0}^{t}{P(s)}/{(T-s)^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}}ds}{{P(t)}/{(T-t)^{\frac{L+1}{L-2}}}}.

Now, the denominator is an increasing function since its derivative is

\left(\frac{-\mu_{2}\|{\mathbf{g}}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}P(t)}{(T-t)^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}}+\frac{L+1}{L-2}\frac{P(t)}{(T-t)^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}}\right)=\frac{P(t)r(t)}{(T-t)^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}}\geq 0,

where the inequality is true since, for all
t\in(0,T)
, from
eq.

80
and
eq.

89
, we have

\frac{L+1}{L-2}-\mu_{2}\|{\mathbf{g}}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}\geq\frac{L+1}{L-2}-\mu_{2}\|{\mathbf{g}}(0)\|_{2}^{L-2}\geq\frac{L+1}{L-2}-\frac{\mu_{2}}{L(L-2)(\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})-\alpha)}\geq 0.

Also, the numerator is a decreasing function since its derivative is

\frac{P(t)r(t)}{(T-t)^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}}-\frac{P(t)r(t)}{(T-t)^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}}-r^{\prime}(t)\int_{0}^{t}\frac{P(s)}{(T-s)^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}}ds=-r^{\prime}(t)\int_{0}^{t}\frac{P(s)}{(T-s)^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}}ds\leq 0,

where the inequality is true since
r(t)
is an increasing function.
Hence,
1-r(t)h(t)
is a decreasing function in
(0,T)
. Now, for the sake of contradiction, let
h^{\prime}(t_{1})=-\epsilon<0
, for some
t_{1}\in(0,T)
. Then, we may assume
1-r(t_{1})h(t_{1})=-\tilde{\epsilon}<0
. Since
1-r(t)h(t)
is a decreasing function in
(0,T)
,
1-r(t)h(t)\leq-\tilde{\epsilon}
, for all
t\geq t_{1}
. Hence,

h(t)-h(t_{1})\leq\int_{t_{1}}^{t}\frac{-\tilde{\epsilon}}{(T-s)}ds=-\tilde{\epsilon}\ln\left(\frac{T-t_{1}}{T-t}\right),\text{ for all }t\in(t_{1},T).

Now, if
t
is chosen sufficiently close to
T
, then
h(t)
will become negative, leading to a contradiction. Hence,
h(t)
is an increasing function in
(0,T)
.

We next compute
\lim_{t\to T}h(t)
. We first show that

\lim_{t\rightarrow T}\int_{0}^{t}P(s)/(T-s)^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}=\infty\text{ and }\lim_{t\rightarrow T}P(t)/(T-t)^{\frac{L+1}{L-2}}=\infty.

From
eq.

89
, we know
\|{\mathbf{g}}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}\leq\|{\mathbf{g}}(0)\|_{2}^{L-2}\leq\frac{1}{L(L-2)(\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})-\alpha)}
. Hence,

\frac{P(t)}{(T-t)^{\frac{L+1}{L-2}}}\geq\frac{e^{-\frac{\mu_{2}}{L(L-2)(\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})-\alpha)}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{ds}{(T-s)}}}{(T-t)^{\frac{L+1}{L-2}}}=\left(\frac{T-t}{T}\right)^{\frac{\mu_{2}}{L(L-2)(\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})-\alpha)}}{(T-t)^{\frac{-L-1}{L-2}}}.

Combining the above inequality with
eq.

80
, we get
\lim_{t\rightarrow T}P(t)/(T-t)^{\frac{L+1}{L-2}}=\infty.
Next,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}{P(s)}/{(T-s)^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}}ds\geq\frac{1}{T^{\frac{\mu_{2}}{L(L-2)(\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})-\alpha)}}}\int_{0}^{t}\left(T-s\right)^{\left(\frac{\mu_{2}}{L(L-2)(\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})-\alpha)}-\frac{2L-1}{L-2}\right)}ds

\displaystyle=\frac{\big((T-t)^{\left(\frac{\mu_{2}}{L(L-2)(\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})-\alpha)}-\frac{L+1}{L-2}\right)}-T^{\left(\frac{\mu_{2}}{L(L-2)(\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})-\alpha)}-\frac{L+1}{L-2}\right)}\big)}{T^{\frac{\mu_{2}}{L(L-2)(\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})-\alpha)}}\left(\frac{L+1}{L-2}-\frac{\mu_{2}}{L(L-2)(\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})-\alpha)}\right)}.

Combining the above inequality with
eq.

80
, we get
\lim_{t\rightarrow T}\int_{0}^{t}P(s)/(T-s)^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}=\infty
.
Thus, using L’Hopital rule, we have

\lim_{t\rightarrow T}h(t)=\lim_{t\rightarrow T}\frac{P(t)/(T-t)^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}}{\frac{P(t)}{(T-t)^{\frac{2L-1}{L-2}}}{\left(\frac{L+1}{L-2}-\mu_{2}\|{\mathbf{g}}(t)\|_{2}^{L-2}\right)}}=\frac{1}{\left(\frac{L+1}{L-2}-\mu_{2}\|{\mathbf{g}}(T)\|_{2}^{L-2}\right)}.

∎

Proof.

of Theorem
11
:
From Lemma
33
, we have

\left\|\bm{\psi}\left(\frac{T_{1}}{\delta^{L-2}}+\frac{T}{\widetilde{\delta}^{L-2}}\left(1-\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{2(L-2)L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}}\right),\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)-b_{\delta}\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{\Delta}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right\|_{2}\leq a_{1}\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{(L+1)\Delta}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}},

for some
T_{1},a_{1}>0
. Define
\bar{\delta}=b_{\delta}\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{\Delta}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}}
, then the above equation implies

\left\|\bm{\psi}\left(\frac{T_{1}}{\delta^{L-2}}+\frac{T}{\widetilde{\delta}^{L-2}}\left(1-\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{2(L-2)L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}}\right),\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)-\bar{\delta}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right\|_{2}\leq a_{2}\bar{\delta}^{L+1},

where
a_{2}\geq a_{1}/b_{\delta}^{L+1}
is a large enough constant. Define
T_{\delta}\coloneqq\frac{T_{1}}{\delta^{L-2}}+\frac{T\big(1-\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{2(L-2)L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}}\big)}{\widetilde{\delta}^{L-2}},

then, using Lemma
31
, for any fixed
\widetilde{T}\in(-\infty,\infty)
, there exists a constant
\widetilde{C}_{1}>0
such that for all sufficiently small
\delta>0
, we have

\left\|\bm{\psi}\left(t+\frac{1/\overline{\delta}^{L-2}}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},\bm{\psi}\left(T_{\delta},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)\right)-\bm{\psi}\left(t+\frac{1/\overline{\delta}^{L-2}}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},\overline{\delta}{{\mathbf{w}}}_{*}\right)\right\|_{2}\leq\widetilde{C}_{1}\overline{\delta},

for all
t\in[-\widetilde{T},\widetilde{T}]
, and from Lemma
32
, there exists a
\widetilde{C}_{2}>0
such that

\left\|{\mathbf{p}}(t)-\bm{\psi}\left(t+\frac{{1}/{\overline{\delta}^{L-2}}}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},\overline{\delta}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right)\right\|_{2}\leq\widetilde{C}_{2}\overline{\delta},\text{ for all }t\in[-\widetilde{T},\widetilde{T}].

Since

\displaystyle\frac{1/\overline{\delta}^{L-2}}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}+T_{\delta}

\displaystyle=\frac{T_{1}}{\delta^{L-2}}+\frac{1/(b_{\delta}^{L-2}\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{(L-2)\Delta}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}})}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}+\frac{T}{\widetilde{\delta}^{L-2}}-\frac{T}{\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{(L-2)\Delta}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{T_{1}}{\delta^{L-2}}+\frac{T}{\widetilde{\delta}^{L-2}}+\left(\frac{1}{b_{\delta}^{L-2}L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}-T\right){\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{-(L-2)\Delta}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}}},

it follows that

\displaystyle\bm{\psi}\left(t+\frac{1/\overline{\delta}^{L-2}}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},\bm{\psi}\left(T_{\delta},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\bm{\psi}\left(t+\frac{T_{1}}{\delta^{L-2}}+\frac{T}{\widetilde{\delta}^{L-2}}+\left(\frac{1}{b_{\delta}^{L-2}L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}-T\right){\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{-(L-2)\Delta}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}}},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right).

Hence, for all
t\in[-\widetilde{T},\widetilde{T}]
and for all sufficiently small
\delta
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\psi}\left(t+\frac{T_{1}}{\delta^{L-2}}+\frac{T}{\widetilde{\delta}^{L-2}}+\left(\frac{1}{b_{\delta}^{L-2}L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}-T\right){\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{-(L-2)\Delta}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}}},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)-{\mathbf{p}}(t)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{\psi}\left(t+\frac{1/\overline{\delta}^{L-2}}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},\bm{\psi}\left(T_{\delta},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)\right)-\bm{\psi}\left(t+\frac{{1}/{\overline{\delta}^{L-2}}}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},\bar{\delta}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right)\right\|_{2}+

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\psi}\left(t+\frac{{1}/{\overline{\delta}^{L-2}}}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})},\bar{\delta}{\mathbf{w}}_{*}\right)-{\mathbf{p}}(t)\right\|_{2}\leq\widetilde{C}_{1}\bar{\delta}+\widetilde{C}_{2}\bar{\delta}\leq\widetilde{C}\delta^{\frac{\Delta}{\Delta+2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}},

where
\widetilde{C}
is a positive constant. The last inequality is true since
b_{\delta}\leq 1
, and
\widetilde{\delta}\leq A_{2}\delta+A_{1}\delta^{L+1}\leq 2A_{2}\delta
, for all sufficiently small
\delta
. Thus, the proof is complete.
∎

Proof.

of Corollary
12
:
Since
{\mathbf{p}}(t)
is bounded for all
t\geq 0
, there exists a constant
B>0
such that
\|{\mathbf{p}}(t)\|_{2}\leq B
, for all
t\geq 0
. Moreover, since
\mathcal{L}(\cdot)
has locally Lipschitz gradient, there exists a constant
\widetilde{A}>0
such that, if
\|{\mathbf{w}}_{1}\|_{2},\|{\mathbf{w}}_{2}\|_{2}\leq 2B
, then

\|\nabla\mathcal{L}({\mathbf{w}}_{1})-\nabla\mathcal{L}({\mathbf{w}}_{2})\|_{2}\leq\widetilde{A}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{2}\|_{2}.

(94)

Since
{\mathbf{p}}^{*}=\lim_{t\to\infty}{\mathbf{p}}(t)
and
\nabla\mathcal{L}({\mathbf{p}}^{*})=\mathbf{0}
, for any
\epsilon\in(0,B)
, we can choose a
T_{\epsilon}
such that

\|{\mathbf{p}}(T_{\epsilon})-{\mathbf{p}}_{*}\|_{2}\leq\epsilon/2\text{ and }\|\nabla\mathcal{L}({\mathbf{p}}(T_{\epsilon}))\|_{2}\leq\epsilon/2.

(95)

Since
T_{\epsilon}
does not depend on
\delta
, from Theorem
11
, for all sufficiently small
\delta>0
,

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\psi}\left(t+\frac{T_{1}}{\delta^{L-2}}+\left(\frac{1/b_{\delta}^{L-2}}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}-T\right){\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{-(L-2)\Delta}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}}}+\frac{T}{\widetilde{\delta}^{L-2}},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)-{\mathbf{p}}(t)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{C}\delta^{\frac{\Delta}{\Delta+2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}}\leq\epsilon/2,\text{ for all }t\in[-T_{\epsilon},T_{\epsilon}].

(96)

Putting
t=T_{\epsilon}
in the above equation, and using
eq.

95
, we get

\left\|\bm{\psi}\left(T_{\delta},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)-{\mathbf{p}}^{*}\right\|_{2}\leq\epsilon,

where
T_{\delta}\coloneqq T_{\epsilon}+\frac{T_{1}}{\delta^{L-2}}+\left(\frac{1/b_{\delta}^{L-2}}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}-T\right){\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{-(L-2)\Delta}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}}}+\frac{T}{\widetilde{\delta}^{L-2}}
. Next, since
\|\bm{\psi}\left(T_{\delta},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)\|_{2}\leq B+\epsilon/2\leq 2B
, using
eq.

94
,
eq.

95
and
eq.

96
, we get

\left\|\nabla\mathcal{L}\left(\bm{\psi}\left(T_{\delta},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)\right)\right\|_{2}\leq\epsilon/2+\widetilde{A}\widetilde{C}\delta^{\frac{\Delta}{\Delta+2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}}\leq\epsilon,

where the final inequality is true for all sufficiently small
\delta>0
. This completes the proof.
∎

Proof.

of Corollary
13
:
Since
\lim_{t\to\infty}{\mathbf{p}}(t)/\|{\mathbf{p}}(t)\|_{2}={\mathbf{p}}^{*}
,
\lim_{t\to\infty}\|{\mathbf{p}}(t)\|_{2}=\infty
, and
\lim_{t\to\infty}\nabla\mathcal{L}({\mathbf{p}}(t))=\mathbf{0}
, we have that for any
\epsilon\in(0,1)
, we can choose a
T_{\epsilon}
such that

{\mathbf{p}}(T_{\epsilon})^{\top}{\mathbf{p}}^{*}/\|{\mathbf{p}}(T_{\epsilon})\|_{2}\geq 1-\epsilon/2,\|{\mathbf{p}}(T_{\epsilon})\|_{2}\geq 1/\epsilon,\text{ and }\|\nabla\mathcal{L}({\mathbf{p}}(T_{\epsilon}))\|_{2}\leq\epsilon/2.

(97)

Let
B_{\epsilon}\coloneqq\max_{t\in[0,T_{\epsilon}]}\|{\mathbf{p}}(t)\|_{2}
. Then, since
\mathcal{L}(\cdot)
has locally Lipschitz gradient, there exists a constant
\widetilde{A}_{\epsilon}>0
such that, if
\|{\mathbf{w}}_{1}\|_{2},\|{\mathbf{w}}_{2}\|_{2}\leq B_{\epsilon}+\epsilon
, then

\|\nabla\mathcal{L}({\mathbf{w}}_{1})-\nabla\mathcal{L}({\mathbf{w}}_{2})\|_{2}\leq\widetilde{A}_{\epsilon}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{2}\|_{2}.

(98)

Here,
\widetilde{A}_{\epsilon}
depends on
\epsilon
. Since
T_{\epsilon}
does not depend on
\delta
, from Theorem
11
, for all sufficiently small
\delta>0
and for all
t\in[-T_{\epsilon},T_{\epsilon}]
,

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\psi}\left(t+\frac{T_{1}}{\delta^{L-2}}+\left(\frac{1/b_{\delta}^{L-2}}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}-T\right)\frac{1}{\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{(L-2)\Delta}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}}}+\frac{T}{\widetilde{\delta}^{L-2}},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)-{\mathbf{p}}(t)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{C}\delta^{\frac{\Delta}{\Delta+2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}}\leq\epsilon/2.

(99)

Putting
t=T_{\epsilon}
in the above equation, and using
eq.

97
, we get

\left\|\bm{\psi}\left(T_{\delta},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)\right\|_{2}\geq\|{\mathbf{p}}(T_{\epsilon})\|_{2}-\widetilde{C}\delta^{\frac{\Delta}{\Delta+2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}}\geq\frac{1}{\epsilon}-\widetilde{C}\delta^{\frac{\Delta}{\Delta+2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}}\geq\frac{1}{2\epsilon},

where
T_{\delta}\coloneqq T_{\epsilon}+\frac{T_{1}}{\delta^{L-2}}+\left(\frac{1/b_{\delta}^{L-2}}{L(L-2)\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}-T\right)\frac{1}{\widetilde{\delta}^{\frac{(L-2)\Delta}{2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})+\Delta}}}+\frac{T}{\widetilde{\delta}^{L-2}}
, and the second inequality is true for all sufficiently small
\delta>0
. We also have

\frac{\bm{\psi}\left(T_{\delta},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)^{\top}{\mathbf{p}}^{*}}{\left\|\bm{\psi}\left(T_{\delta},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)\right\|_{2}}\geq\frac{{\mathbf{p}}(T_{\epsilon})^{\top}{\mathbf{p}}_{*}-\widetilde{C}\delta^{\frac{\Delta}{\Delta+2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}}}{\left\|\bm{\psi}\left(T_{\delta},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)\right\|_{2}}\geq\frac{(1-\epsilon/2)\|{\mathbf{p}}(T_{\epsilon})\|_{2}-\widetilde{C}\delta^{\frac{\Delta}{\Delta+2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{p}}(T_{\epsilon})\right\|_{2}+\widetilde{C}\delta^{\frac{\Delta}{\Delta+2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}}}\geq 1-\epsilon,

where the first inequality uses
eq.

99
. The second inequality uses
eq.

99
and
eq.

97
. The final inequality is true for all sufficiently small
\delta>0
. Next, since
\|\bm{\psi}\left(T_{\delta},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)\|_{2}\leq B_{\epsilon}+\epsilon/2
, using
eq.

98
and
eq.

97
, we get

\left\|\nabla\mathcal{L}\left(\bm{\psi}\left(T_{\delta},\delta{\mathbf{w}}_{0}\right)\right)\right\|_{2}\leq\epsilon/2+\widetilde{A}_{\epsilon}\widetilde{C}\delta^{\frac{\Delta}{\Delta+2L^{2}\mathcal{N}({\mathbf{w}}_{*})}}\leq\epsilon,

where the final inequality is true for all sufficiently small
\delta>0
.
∎

Appendix D
Proof Omitted from
Section

4

Proof.

of Lemma
17
:
We first compute the gradient of
\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{x}};{\mathbf{W}}_{1},\cdots,{\mathbf{W}}_{L})
with respect to
{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j]\text{ and }{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:]
. Let

\displaystyle\phi_{0}({\mathbf{x}})={\mathbf{x}}\text{ and }\phi_{l}({\mathbf{x}})=\sigma({\mathbf{W}}_{l}\phi_{l-1}({\mathbf{x}})),\text{ for all }l\geq 1,\text{ and }

\displaystyle\psi_{L}({\mathbf{z}})={\mathbf{W}}_{L}{\mathbf{z}},\text{ and }\psi_{l}({\mathbf{z}})=\psi_{l+1}(\sigma({\mathbf{W}}_{l}{\mathbf{z}})),\text{ for all }l\leq L-1.

Then

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{x}};{\mathbf{W}}_{1},\cdots,{\mathbf{W}}_{L})

\displaystyle=\psi_{l+1}\left({\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}\sigma({\mathbf{W}}_{l}\phi_{l-1}({\mathbf{x}}))\right)

\displaystyle=\psi_{l+1}\left(\sum_{p=1}^{k_{l}}{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,p]\sigma({\mathbf{W}}_{l}[p,:]^{\top}\phi_{l-1}({\mathbf{x}}))\right),

which implies

\frac{d\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{x}};{\mathbf{W}}_{1},\cdots,{\mathbf{W}}_{L})}{d{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j]}=\nabla\psi_{l+1}\left(\sum_{p=1}^{k_{l}}{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,p]\sigma({\mathbf{W}}_{l}[p,:]^{\top}\phi_{l-1}({\mathbf{x}}))\right)\sigma({\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:]^{\top}\phi_{l-1}({\mathbf{x}})),

and

\displaystyle\frac{d\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{x}};{\mathbf{W}}_{1},\cdots,{\mathbf{W}}_{L})}{d{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:]}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j]^{\top}\nabla\psi_{l+1}\left(\sum_{p=1}^{k_{l}}{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,p]\sigma({\mathbf{W}}_{l}[p,:]^{\top}\phi_{l-1}({\mathbf{x}}))\right)\sigma^{\prime}({\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:]^{\top}\phi_{l-1}({\mathbf{x}}))\phi_{l-1}({\mathbf{x}}).

Now, note that, if the weights are bounded, then there exists a
K>0
such that

\left\|\frac{d\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{x}};{\mathbf{W}}_{1},\cdots,{\mathbf{W}}_{L})}{d{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j]}\right\|_{2}\leq K\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:]\|_{2},\left\|\frac{d\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{x}};{\mathbf{W}}_{1},\cdots,{\mathbf{W}}_{L})}{d{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:]}\right\|_{2}\leq K\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j]\|_{2}.

(100)

The first inequality follows from Cauchy-Schwartz inequality and since
|\sigma({\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:]^{\top}\phi_{l-1}({\mathbf{x}}))|\leq K_{1}|{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:]^{\top}\phi_{l-1}({\mathbf{x}})|
, for some
K_{1}>0
, which follows from
\sigma(\cdot)
being locally Lipshitz and
\sigma(0)=0
. The second inequality follows from Cauchy-Schwartz inequality.

Now, let
({\mathbf{W}}_{1}(t),\cdots,{\mathbf{W}}_{L}(t))
be the solution of

\dot{{\mathbf{W}}}_{i}=-\nabla_{{\mathbf{W}}_{i}}\mathcal{L}({\mathbf{W}}_{1},\cdots,{\mathbf{W}}_{L}),\text{ for all }i\in[L].

If
{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j],{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:]\in{\mathbf{w}}_{z}
, for some
l\in[L-1]
and
j\in[k_{l}]
, then
\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j](0)\|_{2}=0=\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:](0)\|_{2}
. Also, note that

\displaystyle\dot{{\mathbf{W}}}_{l+1}[:,j]=-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ell^{\prime}\left(\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{x}}_{i};{\mathbf{W}}_{1},\cdots,{\mathbf{W}}_{L}),y_{i}\right)\frac{d\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{x}}_{i};{\mathbf{W}}_{1},\cdots,{\mathbf{W}}_{L})}{d{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j]},\mbox{ and}

\displaystyle\dot{{\mathbf{W}}}_{l}[j,:]=-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ell^{\prime}\left(\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{x}}_{i};{\mathbf{W}}_{1},\cdots,{\mathbf{W}}_{L}),y_{i}\right)\frac{d\mathcal{H}({\mathbf{x}}_{i};{\mathbf{W}}_{1},\cdots,{\mathbf{W}}_{L})}{d{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:]}.

Let
T\in(0,\infty)
, then for all
t\in[-T,T]
, we can assume
({\mathbf{W}}_{1}(t),\cdots,{\mathbf{W}}_{L}(t))
are bounded. Since the output of the neural network and the loss function are bounded for bounded input, from
eq.

100
, there exists a
B>0
such that, for all
t\in[0,T]
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j]\|_{2}^{2}}{dt}\leq\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j]\|_{2}\|\dot{{\mathbf{W}}}_{l+1}[:,j]\|_{2}\leq B\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j]\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:]\|_{2},\mbox{ and}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:]\|_{2}^{2}}{dt}\leq\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:]\|_{2}\|\dot{{\mathbf{W}}}_{l}[j,:]\|_{2}\leq B\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:]\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j]\|_{2}.

Adding the above two equations, and since
2xy\leq x^{2}+y^{2}
, we get

\frac{d\left(\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j]\|_{2}^{2}+\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:]\|_{2}^{2}\right)}{dt}\leq 2B\left(\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j]\|_{2}^{2}+\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:]\|_{2}^{2}\right).

(101)

Integrating the above equation from
0
to
t\in[0,T]
, we get

\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j](t)\|_{2}^{2}+\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:](t)\|_{2}^{2}\leq(\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j](0)\|_{2}^{2}+\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:](0)\|_{2}^{2})e^{2tB}.

Since
\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j](0)\|_{2}=0=\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:](0)\|_{2}
, the above equation implies
\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j](t)\|_{2}=0=\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:](t)\|_{2}
, for all
t\in[0,T]
. Similarly, there exists a
B>0
such that, for all
t\in[-T,0]
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j]\|_{2}^{2}}{dt}\geq-\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j]\|_{2}\|\dot{{\mathbf{W}}}_{l+1}[:,j]\|_{2}\geq-B\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j]\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:]\|_{2},\mbox{ and}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:]\|_{2}^{2}}{dt}\geq-\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:]\|_{2}\|\dot{{\mathbf{W}}}_{l}[j,:]\|_{2}\geq-B\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:]\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j]\|_{2}.

Adding the above two equation gives us

\frac{d\left(\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j]\|_{2}^{2}+\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:]\|_{2}^{2}\right)}{dt}\geq-2B\left(\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j]\|_{2}^{2}+\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:]\|_{2}^{2}\right).

(102)

Integrating the above equation from
t\in[-T,0]
to
0
, we get

\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j](t)\|_{2}^{2}+\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:](t)\|_{2}^{2}\leq(\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j](0)\|_{2}^{2}+\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:](0)\|_{2}^{2})e^{-2tB},

which implies
\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l+1}[:,j](t)\|_{2}=0=\|{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:](t)\|_{2}
, for all
t\in[-T,0]
.
∎

Proof.

of Theorem
19
:
Note that
{\mathbf{w}}_{z}
is a zero-preserving subset. If
{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:]\in{\mathbf{w}}_{z}
, for some
l\in[L-1]
and
j\in[k_{l}]
, then
\|\overline{{\mathbf{W}}}_{l}[j,:]\|_{2}=0
, and from Lemma
18
,
\|\overline{{\mathbf{W}}}_{l+1}[:,j]\|_{2}=0
, which implies
{{\mathbf{W}}}_{l+1}[:,j]\in{\mathbf{w}}_{z}
. Similarly, if
{{\mathbf{W}}}_{l+1}[:,j]\in{\mathbf{w}}_{z}
, then
{\mathbf{W}}_{l}[j,:]\in{\mathbf{w}}_{z}
. Hence,
{\mathbf{w}}_{z}
is a zero-preserving subset.

Throughout this proof, let
\overline{{\mathbf{w}}}
denote the vector containing the entries of
(\overline{{\mathbf{W}}}_{1},\cdots,\overline{{\mathbf{W}}}_{L})
, and
\overline{{\mathbf{w}}}_{z}
be the vector containing the entries of
(\overline{{\mathbf{W}}}_{1},\cdots,\overline{{\mathbf{W}}}_{L})
that belong to
{\mathbf{w}}_{z}
. Now, consider the case when
\mathcal{H}
is two-homogeneous. From Lemma
9
, we know
